Method: The authors created a high-fidelity HDR dataset of 1K indoor scene image pairs with physically manipulated light probes (on/off). They annotated regions like cast shadows and metallic surfaces, developed two new evaluation metrics compensating for AI artifacts (e.g., white balance adjustments), and tested state-of-the-art image editing models against this benchmark.

Result: Model performance varied significantly, with specular highlights showing smaller differences. Top models aligned well with physics but still had limitations, particularly in underlit regions. VLMs proved inadequate for pixel-level light transport analysis despite success in macroscopic plausibility checks.

Conclusion: The 3DLP benchmark reveals critical gaps in current models' lighting understanding, especially in complex regions, while demonstrating the limitations of VLMs for fine-grained analysis. Public release of the dataset aims to drive improvements in physically accurate image editing.

Abstract: While recent advancements in generative image editing models have achieved stunning visual fidelity, it remains an open question whether these systems possess an intrinsic knowledge of real-world lighting. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate high-level plausibility of perceptual light transport on curated internet imagery, using VLMs or human judgement, or they rely on synthetically generated datasets. In this work, we introduce the 3D-anchored Light Probe (3DLP) benchmark, for which we have captured a new high-fidelity HDR dataset of real-world lighting changes. The dataset consists of 1K image pairs of diverse indoor scenery in which light probes are physically turned on and off. To allow for a granular performance analysis, we annotated specific image regions such as cast shadows or metallic surfaces. With this data, we evaluate a range of state-of-the-art image editing models by measuring how well their light probe edits align with reality. The evaluation uses two new scores to compensate for AI-generated photographic effects, such as adjusted white balance. Our results show that the overall performance of models differs considerably, with differences slightly less pronounced for specular highlights. The best image editing models are remarkably consistent with real-world physics, however, they still leave room for improvement. We observe that image regions that receive less light from the light probe are more prone to errors for all models. Furthermore, building on their success in evaluating macroscopic lighting plausibility, we test VLMs on our task but find that they are unsuitable for pixel-level light transport analysis. We will make the benchmark, together with the real-world dataset, publicly available to encourage future research on this topic.

</details>


### [38] [LiveEdit: Towards Real-Time Diffusion-Based Streaming Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26740)
*Xinyu Wang,Chongbo Zhao,Fangneng Zhan,Yue Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的流式视频编辑框架，通过三阶段蒸馏管道和AR导向的掩码缓存技术，实现了实时、稳定的视频编辑，推理速度达到12.66 FPS，适合交互式和增强现实应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频编辑面临两个核心挑战：1) 在时间上保持背景稳定和非编辑区域一致性；2) 实现实时交互场景所需的低延迟。现有流式视频生成方法主要针对合成任务，无法直接应用于编辑，因为编辑需要严格的保存要求和区域特定控制。

Method: 提出三阶段蒸馏管道，将强大的双向基础模型的编辑能力逐步转移到高效的单向流式编辑器。引入AR导向的掩码缓存技术，跨帧重用区域相关计算，减少冗余处理。实现因果的、逐帧编辑框架，确保内容保存和实时响应。

Result: 在流式视频编辑基准测试中达到最先进的视觉质量，推理速度提升至12.66 FPS，显著优于现有流式基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法在不牺牲视觉保真度的情况下实现了稳定的长期编辑，适合交互式应用和增强现实应用，为实时流式视频编辑提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Streaming video editing has made rapid progress, yet practical deployment is still limited by two core issues: maintaining stable backgrounds and non-edited regions over time, and achieving the low latency required for real-time interactive scenarios. Meanwhile, recent streaming video generation methods are mostly developed for synthesis and cannot be directly applied to editing due to the strict preservation requirement and region-specific control. In this work, we present a novel streaming video editing framework that performs causal, frame-by-frame editing with strong content preservation and real-time responsiveness. Our key design is a three-stage distillation pipeline that progressively transfers editing capability from a powerful bidirectional foundation model to an efficient unidirectional streaming editor, enabling stable long-horizon edits without sacrificing visual fidelity. To further support real-time deployment, we introduce an AR-oriented mask cache that reuses region-related computation across frames, substantially reducing redundant processing and accelerating inference. Finally, we establish a dedicated benchmark for streaming video editing. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art visual quality among streaming baselines while drastically boosting inference speed to 12.66 FPS, making it suitable for interactive and augmented reality applications.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波雷达点云存在稀疏性、噪声干扰及结构不完整等问题，严重影响目标检测与跟踪等下游感知任务的性能表现。

Result: 实验验证该方法显著改善点云质量，在复杂环境中有效提升目标检测精度与感知模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本研究为多传感器点云生成及智能感知系统提供了实用解决方案，解决了雷达点云质量缺陷问题并优化了感知任务表现。

Abstract: Point clouds are an important carrier of three-dimensional spatial information, and their quality directly affects the performance of downstream perception tasks such as object detection and tracking. However, millimeter-wave radar point clouds are typically sparse, noisy, and structurally incomplete. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a multimodal point cloud generation method based on vision-radar fusion. The proposed method leverages image semantic information to impose structural constraints and achieve spatial alignment for radar point clouds, while incorporating a sparse completion strategy to enhance point density and recover missing structures. The generated point clouds are further evaluated in object detection and tracking tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method effectively improves point cloud quality and enhances the detection accuracy and robustness of perception models in complex environments, providing a practical solution for multisensor point cloud generation and intelligent perception systems.

</details>


### [40] [Capacity-Controlled Multi-View Stylization of 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26754)
*Zhihao Wen,Yixin Yang,Bojian Wu,Yang Zhou,Dani Lischinski,Daniel Cohen-Or,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最优传输的3D高斯溅射多视角风格化方法，通过容量控制和跨视图匹配引导解决了现有方法风格不一致的问题，显著提升了多视角风格一致性和3D风格化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯溅射(3DGS)虽然为新颖视角合成提供了高效显式表示，但在多视角风格化中保持风格一致性仍具挑战性。现有3D风格化方法通常独立地对每个渲染视图应用2D特征匹配损失，导致风格分配不稳定、特征重用过多以及跨视图一致性有限。

Method: 提出基于最优传输的容量控制框架进行3DGS多视角风格化。将局部风格匹配重新表述为半平衡最优传输问题，引入可调强度的显式列容量约束来缓解多对一匹配问题并实现可控的风格特征分配。同时引入跨视图匹配引导来约束场景内容与风格模式之间的对应关系，并添加几何正则化来增强基础3DGS，使优化后的高斯基元能够表示更精细的纹理。

Result: 实验证明该方法显著改善了多视角风格一致性，产生了稳定、富有表现力的3D风格化效果，同时保留了场景的核心语义结构。

Conclusion: 通过最优传输理论和容量控制机制，有效解决了3DGS多视角风格化中的风格一致性问题，为3D内容创作提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides an efficient and explicit representation for novel view synthesis, enforcing stylistic coherence across viewpoints remains challenging. Existing 3D stylization methods typically apply 2D feature-matching losses independently per rendered view, which leads to unstable style allocation, many-to-one feature reuse, and limited cross-view consistency. We propose a capacity-controlled framework for multi-view stylization of 3DGS, grounded in optimal transport. Specifically, we reformulate local style matching as a semi-balanced optimal transport problem. By introducing explicit column-capacity constraints with tunable strength, our formulation mitigates many-to-one matching and enables controllable allocation of style features. This transport-based objective provides a principled mechanism for balancing feature coverage and stylistic diversity while maintaining stable correspondences across viewpoints. To further enhance cross-view coherence, we incorporate a novel cross-view matching guidance to constrain correspondences between scene content and style patterns. In addition, we introduce several geometric regularizations to enhance the vanilla 3DGS, thereby enabling optimized Gaussian primitives to represent finer-grained textures during stylization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly improves multi-view stylistic consistency and produces stable, expressive 3D stylizations while preserving the core semantic structure of the scene.

</details>


### [41] [ProtoKV: Streaming Video Understanding under Delayed Query with Summary-State Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26762)
*Le Tu Ngoc Minh,Jinyeong Lim,Dongsu Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoKV是一种针对流式视频理解的恒定内存占用解决方案，通过将远历史压缩为语义-空间原型库来解决延迟查询问题，在长延迟场景下相比基线方法准确率提升高达12.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式视频理解需要在严格GPU内存和查询延迟预算下异步回答查询，而延迟查询问题使得决定性线索可能短暂出现后被后续更新稀释或驱逐，这是当前token保留方法面临的关键挑战。

Method: ProtoKV保持精确的近窗口KV缓存，将较旧内容聚合到带有残差统计的语义-空间原型库中，查询时每个原型通过有界伪token接口暴露，与标准注意力机制完全兼容。

Result: 在匹配预算和可比的查询时间成本下，ProtoKV在SVU基准测试的长延迟场景中准确率提升高达12.5%，且随着查询延迟增加，性能增益持续增长。

Conclusion: ProtoKV通过恒定内存占用的原型库设计有效解决了流式视频理解中的延迟查询问题，为实时视频理解系统提供了更高效、更准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Streaming video understanding (SVU) must answer queries that arrive asynchronously while visual tokens stream continuously under strict GPU-memory and query-time latency budgets. A key challenge is delayed query: decisive cues may appear briefly, yet many subsequent updates occur before the query arrives, increasing the risk that those cues are evicted or diluted under bounded memory. We propose ProtoKV, a constant-footprint SVU memory that represents far history as a fixed-capacity summary state rather than retaining token instances. ProtoKV keeps an exact near-window KV cache and aggregates older content into a semantic-spatial prototype bank with residual statistics. At query time, each prototype is exposed through a bounded pseudo-token interface that is drop-in compatible with standard attention. Under matched budgets and comparable query-time cost, ProtoKV improves accuracy by up to 12.5 points over token-retention baselines on SVU benchmarks in the long-delay regime, with gains that grow as query delay increases.

</details>


### [42] [Calibrated Harmonic Overlaid Implicit Neural Representations for Multi-Dimensional Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26763)
*Honghang Chen,Xiujun Zhang,Xiaoli Sun,Mingqing Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CHOIR方法，通过协调谐波叠加（CHS）替代传统函数组合以解决隐式神经表示（INR）的深度优化不稳定问题，并引入感知光谱校准（PSC）利用自然图像幂律光谱先验缓解频谱偏差，实验证明其在多维数据恢复任务中性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于周期性激活函数的INR方法依赖函数组合机制，导致网络深度增加时优化不稳定；同时缺乏物理先验导致频谱偏差问题难以解决。

Method: 提出CHOIR框架：1）使用协调谐波叠加（CHS）替代传统函数组合，保证深度扩展时的优化稳定性；2）设计感知光谱校准（PSC），嵌入自然图像幂律光谱先验，将全局固定频谱调整为符合物理规律的log-uniform分布。

Result: 在多种多维数据恢复任务中，CHOIR方法显著优于当前最先进方法，验证了其在优化稳定性和频谱偏差缓解方面的有效性。

Conclusion: CHOIR通过结合谐波叠加机制与物理光谱先验，有效解决了INR的深度优化不稳定性和频谱偏差问题，为多维数据建模提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Implicit neural representation (INR) has emerged as a powerful prior for multi-dimensional data (e.g., multispectral images and videos). However, most INR methods employing periodic activation functions (e.g., Sine) predominantly rely on function composition. This mechanism introduces optimization instability as network depth increases, thereby limiting their performance. Meanwhile, these methods fail to incorporate proper physical priors to effectively alleviate spectrum bias. To address these issues, inspired by the commonalities between deep periodic networks and generalized Fourier series, we propose a novel Calibrated Harmonic Overlaid Implicit Neural Representation (CHOIR). Specifically, we utilize Coordinated Harmonic Superposition (CHS) to replace the conventional function composition used in most INRs, thereby ensuring optimization stability when scaling network depth. Furthermore, we introduce a Perceptual Spectrum Calibration (PSC) to mitigate spectrum bias. This calibration embeds the ubiquitous power-law spectrum prior of natural images and adjusts the globally fixed spectrum towards a physically plausible log-uniform distribution. Extensive experiments on various multidimensional data recovery problems demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/chorl0229/CHOIR.

</details>


### [43] [Anatomy-Guided Residual Motion Diffusion for Controllable 4D Cardiac MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26764)
*Yiheng Cao,Gustavo Andrade-Miranda,Jiatian Zhang,Lingxiao Zhao,Xin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种可控的4D医学图像生成框架，通过半监督变分自编码器和级联潜在扩散模型实现解剖结构与时间动态的解耦，有效解决数据标注不足、设备域偏移及隐私限制问题。在心脏MRI数据集上验证了其高可控性和时间连贯性，合成数据显著提升跨厂商分割模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D医学图像AI模型开发受限于标注数据稀缺、设备间域偏移及隐私保护要求，亟需可扩展的解剖一致性数据增强方案。

Method: 1. 半监督变分自编码器联合学习解剖体积潜在表示与分割掩码；2. 级联潜在扩散模型解耦静态解剖结构（基于临床先验条件生成）与时间动态（残差运动估计）；3. 确保4D序列严格时间连贯性。

Result: 在心脏电影MRI任务中：1. 静态解剖可控性达Pearson r>0.8；2. 时间连贯性FVD=288.08；3. 跨厂商泛化实验中，合成数据增强使nnU-Net左心室分割Dice提升2.8%，Hausdorff距离减少5.4mm。

Conclusion: 框架提供可扩展、可控的4D医学图像合成解决方案，有效缓解标注数据不足和跨设备变异问题，为鲁棒模型开发奠定基础。

Abstract: Developing robust artificial intelligence models for 4D (3D + time) medical imaging is constrained by limited annotated data, inter-device domain shifts, and privacy restrictions. To address this, we propose a 4D controllable generative framework for anatomically consistent data augmentation. A semi-supervised variational autoencoder learns a compact latent representation of anatomical volumes while jointly predicting aligned segmentation masks in a unified framework. Anatomical structure is then disentangled from temporal dynamics through a cascaded latent diffusion model (LDM). A static LDM generates subject-specific anatomy conditioned on clinical priors (diagnosis and volumes measures) and a subsequent motion LDM estimates residual latent motions, ensuring strict temporal coherence across the 4D sequence. The proposed approach was evaluated on cine cardiac MRI as a representative 4D imaging application. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate high controllability of static anatomy (Pearson r > 0.8) and strong temporal coherence (FVD = 288.08). In cross-vendor generalization experiments, augmenting training sets with synthetic 4D sequences significantly improves downstream segmentation performance. Using nnU-Net, the proposed augmentation strategy improves the average Dice score by 1.4% and reduces the Hausdorff Distance by 3.0mm compared to training on real data alone, for the left ventricle, Dice improves by 2.8% with a 5.4mm reduction in boundary error. Overall, this framework provides a scalable and controllable solution for 4D medical image synthesis, supporting the development of more robust models with limited annotations and cross-vendor variability. Code available on https://github.com/cyiheng/4DCardiacMRISynthesis.

</details>


### [44] [LearniBridge: Learnable Calibration of Feature Caching for Diffusion Models Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26778)
*Xuyue Huang,Zhe Chen,Wang Shen,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LearniBridge，一个基于可学习校准的特征缓存机制，通过轻量级LoRA更新桥接多个时间步，在保持生成质量的同时实现扩散Transformer的显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer(DiTs)在图像和视频生成领域取得重大进展，但计算成本过高。现有特征缓存方法虽然实现简单，但在高加速比下存在严重的误差累积问题，限制了加速效果。

Method: 作者发现最优校准更新具有跨不同提示的共享低秩子空间特性。基于此结构洞察，提出LearniBridge：一个可学习的校准机制，通过轻量级LoRA更新桥接多个时间步，仅需3-5个训练样本即可实现有效校准。

Result: 在图像和视频生成任务上，LearniBridge在FLUX、HunyuanVideo和WAN2.1上分别实现了5.87×、5.75×和4.10×的加速。在WAN2.1上，相比之前SOTA在4.10×加速下VBench提升了1.28%。

Conclusion: LearniBridge通过可学习的特征校准机制有效解决了特征缓存中的误差累积问题，在大幅降低计算成本的同时保持了生成质量，为扩散Transformer的高效推理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have driven substantial progress in image and video generation but suffer from prohibitive computational costs. Feature caching accelerates inference by reusing intermediate representations. Existing methods rely on historical features for implementation simplicity, yet suffer from severe error accumulation at high acceleration ratios. To address this limitation, we investigate the nature of the requisite feature correction. We demonstrate that the optimal calibration update is characterized by a shared low-rank subspace across diverse prompts. Guided by this structural insight, we propose LearniBridge, a learnable calibration mechanism for feature caching that bridges multiple timesteps through lightweight LoRA updates. This mechanism enables effective calibration requiring only 3-5 training samples. Extensive experiments on image and video generation show that LearniBridge achieves up to $5.87\times$, $5.75\times$, and $4.10\times$ acceleration on FLUX, HunyuanVideo, and WAN2.1, respectively. On WAN2.1, it improves VBench by 1.28% over the previous SOTA at $4.10\times$ acceleration. Our code is available at https://github.com/Iiiiiiirene/LearniBridge.

</details>


### [45] [Event-based Gaze Control System for Accurate Real-time Spin Estimation in Professional Ball Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26780)
*Yunpu Hu,Fabian Schilling,Valentina Cavinato,Asude Aydin,Agis Politis,Ricardo Tapiador Morales,Kirk Y. W. Scheper,Peter Dürr,Naoya Takahashi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于事件相机的主动视觉系统，结合高速云台镜和可调焦长焦镜头，实现对球类运动中未修改球的实时旋转测量。离线方法s-CMax在静态球上达到2.1%幅度和4.0°轴误差，在线方法在乒乓球实战中实现8.8%幅度误差、6.4°轴误差、3ms延迟和750Hz吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相机因球体尺寸小、高速运动和快速旋转导致实时旋转估计困难，影响球类运动中轨迹分析的准确性。

Method: 采用事件相机（高时间分辨率、低运动模糊）结合高速云台镜和可调焦长焦镜头；混合追踪方法（2D事件检测居中+3D定位系统重初始化）；离线对比度最大化方法s-CMax；在线不确定性感知CNN结合GPU加速对比度优化。

Result: 静态球测试中s-CMax达到2.1%幅度误差和4.0°轴误差；乒乓球实战中实现8.8%幅度误差、6.4°轴误差、3ms延迟和750Hz吞吐量。

Conclusion: 该系统有效解决了高速旋转球的实时追踪与高精度旋转测量问题，在专业乒乓球比赛中验证了其可靠性，为体育分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: Spin plays a crucial role in many ball sports due to its effect on the trajectory of the ball. Vision-based estimation of the ball's spin during a game with conventional cameras is challenging due to the ball's small size, high speed, and fast rotation. To address these challenges, we propose an event-based active vision system that can track unmodified balls and measure their spin in real-time. The system consists of an event camera for its high temporal resolution and minimal motion blur, high-speed pan/tilt galvanometer mirrors to keep the ball in the field of view, and a low-latency focus-tunable telephoto lens to increase the spatial resolution on the ball and keep it in focus. To track the ball, we use a hybrid approach that combines 2D event-based detection for centering and 3D positions from a ball localization system for re-initialization. For high-accuracy spin estimation, we propose an offline method that performs contrast maximization on the sphere (s-CMax). This method achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on static balls across multiple sports (table tennis, baseball, tennis, and golf), with mean magnitude and axis errors of 2.1% and 4.0 degrees, respectively. We then develop a low-latency online method for table tennis as a case study in real-time applications. This method uses an uncertainty-aware convolutional neural network trained on pseudo-ground-truth spin labels from the offline approach, combined with a GPU-accelerated batch implementation of contrast maximization for refinement. We demonstrate reliable tracking and spin estimation with a three-view setup during professional table tennis matches, with high accuracy (8.8% magnitude and 6.4 degrees axis mismatch), 3 ms latency, and 750 Hz throughput.

</details>


### [46] [ReasonCLIP-58M: Visually Grounded Commonsense Reasoning Supervision for CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26794)
*Sicheng Zhang,Muzammal Naseer,Binzhu Xie,Naufal Suryanto,Shi Qiu,Jamal Bentahar,Naveed Akhtar,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReasonCLIP-58M是一个持续预训练框架，通过两阶段策略将大规模推理监督集成到CLIP风格模型中，提升了视觉基础常识推理和组合推理能力，同时保持零样本检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP及其变体在多模态系统中被广泛采用，但预训练主要由描述性图像-文本对齐主导。随着下游应用对视觉基础常识推理和组合推理的需求增加，需要验证CLIP风格编码器是否能在不改变架构的情况下支持此类推理。

Method: 提出ReasonCLIP-58M持续预训练框架，采用两阶段策略：第一阶段渐进集成推理信号同时保留描述性对齐，第二阶段进行类别结构化推理监督。构建了ReasonLite-42M（开放形式、可视觉验证的推理说明）、ReasonPro-16M（类别特定推理监督）和RCLIP-Bench（诊断评估基准）三个数据集。

Result: 训练了ReasonCLIP系列模型，改善了视觉基础常识和组合推理能力，同时增强了零样本检索性能。作为LLaVA-NeXT等多模态大语言模型的视觉编码器，在不增加额外推理成本的情况下提供了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 结构化推理监督能够增强CLIP风格视觉表示的表达能力，证明了持续预训练框架在不改变架构的情况下提升模型推理能力的有效性。

Abstract: CLIP and its variants are widely adopted visual backbones in multimodal systems, but their pretraining remains dominated by descriptive image-text alignment. As downstream applications increasingly demand visually grounded commonsense inference and compositional reasoning, it remains unclear whether CLIP-style encoders can support such reasoning without architectural changes. To address this, we present ReasonCLIP-58M, a continual pretraining framework that integrates large-scale reasoning supervision into CLIP-style models through our two-stage strategy, which progressively integrates reasoning signals while preserving descriptive alignment, followed by category-structured reasoning supervision. To support this framework, we construct two complementary datasets and a benchmark: ReasonLite-42M, with open-form, visually verifiable reasoning captions; ReasonPro-16M, with category-specific reasoning supervision; and RCLIP-Bench for diagnostic evaluation of visually grounded reasoning. We train a family of ReasonCLIP that improves visually grounded commonsense and compositional reasoning while also enhancing zero-shot retrieval performance. As a drop-in visual encoder for multimodal large language models such as LLaVA-NeXT, ReasonCLIP delivers consistent gains without additional inference cost, demonstrating that structured reasoning supervision enhances the expressive capacity of CLIP-style visual representations. All datasets, models, and training code are available at https://github.com/RISys-Lab/ReasonCLIP.

</details>


### [47] [NaviCache: Test-Time Self-Calibration Caching for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26795)
*Zheqi Lv,Zhibo Zhu,Jinke Wang,Qi Tian,Shengyu Zhang,Zhengyu Chen,Chengxi Zang,Zhou Zhao,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NaviCache提出了一种基于惯性导航系统的测试时自校准方法，通过双状态估计架构解决视频扩散模型计算成本高及现有加速方法局限性问题，在多个主流模型上实现更精准的计算跳过和优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型受限于高计算成本，现有离线校准方法依赖校准数据且耗时，离线免校准方法易受观测噪声影响并忽略扩散轨迹内在动量特性。

Method: 提出即插即用测试时自校准方法NaviCache，将特征演化建模为惯性导航系统问题，采用双状态估计架构自适应跟踪特征变化率与潜在漂移，结合时间依赖噪声调度和不确定性感知测量更新机制实现理论保证的错误边界计算跳过。

Result: 在HunyuanVideo、Wan和Open-Sora系列模型上的实验表明，NaviCache在计算跳过决策准确性方面表现更优，并达到卓越的综合性能指标。

Conclusion: NaviCache通过创新性地将扩散过程与惯性导航理论结合，有效克服了传统加速方法的缺陷，为视频扩散模型提供了高效可靠的计算优化方案。

Abstract: Video Diffusion Models (VDMs) is constrained by immense computational costs. While offline calibration-based acceleration suffers from calibration data dependency, prohibitive calibration duration, and susceptibility to distribution shifts, offline calibration-free methods eliminate these hurdles. However, since they rely on instantaneous zero-order approximations where the mapping between input and output differences varies in real-time, they are susceptible to observational noise and ignore the intrinsic momentum within the diffusion trajectory. In this paper, we propose NaviCache, a plug-and-play test-time self-calibration method re-conceptualizing feature evolution as an Inertial Navigation System (INS) problem. NaviCache bridges the fundamental domain gap and the non-stationary nature of diffusion by modeling the relative coupling between input and output variations. We introduce a dual-state estimation architecture that adaptively tracks the feature change ratio and its latent drift, initialized via a specialized Initial Alignment phase. By integrating a time-dependent noise schedule with an uncertainty-aware Measurement Update mechanism, NaviCache provides a theoretically grounded mechanism for error-bounded computation skipping. Extensive experiments on the HunyuanVideo, Wan, and Open-Sora series demonstrate that NaviCache exhibits more accurate error judgment for computation skipping and achieves outstanding comprehensive performance.

</details>


### [48] [Multi-modality Image Fusion under Adverse Weather: Mask-Guided Feature Restoration and Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26812)
*Xilai Li,Xiaosong Li,Haishu Tan,Tao Ye,Huafeng Li,Hongbin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种掩码引导的多模态图像融合方法，通过整合特征恢复和交互机制，有效解决了恶劣天气条件下图像退化问题，在多个数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态图像融合通过利用不同模态的互补信息增强场景表示，但恶劣天气导致图像严重退化，破坏特征表示。现有方法在恶劣天气条件下难以进行有效的表示学习，限制了实际性能，需要同时解决特征恢复和跨模态互补性问题。

Method: 提出掩码引导的多模态图像融合方法，包含：(1)引入"伪地面真实值"简化训练，促进特征学习；(2)设计基于融合结果与源图像映射关系的掩码生成机制，量化各模态相对贡献；(3)掩码引导的跨模态交叉注意力机制，选择性关注信息特征；(4)掩码引导学习策略和任务耦合的退化感知学习策略，平衡特征恢复与交互。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上进行大量实验，该方法在视觉质量、定量指标和下游任务方面均超越最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了恶劣天气条件下多模态图像融合的挑战，通过掩码引导机制实现了特征恢复与跨模态交互的平衡，显著提升了融合性能。

Abstract: Multi-modality image fusion (MMIF) enhances scene representation by exploiting complementary cues from different modalities. Adverse weather, however, causes significant image degradation, disrupting feature representation and requiring simultaneous feature restoration and cross-modal complementarity. Existing methods often struggle with effective representation learning under such conditions, limiting their practical performance. To address these challenges, we propose a mask-guided MMIF method that integrates feature restoration and interaction. We first introduce "Pseudo Ground Truth" to simplify training, promoting faster and more effective feature learning. Then, we design a mask generation mechanism based on the mapping relationship between the fused result and the source images, quantifying the relative contribution of each modality during the fusion process. By incorporating the proposed mask-guided cross-modal cross-attention mechanism, the network is encouraged to selectively attend to informative features during modality interaction, mitigating the risk of overfitting to the static distribution of the "Pseudo Ground Truth". Additionally, we propose a mask-guided learning strategy and a task-coupled degradation-aware learning strategy to balance feature restoration and interaction. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that our method surpasses state-of-the-art approaches in visual quality, quantitative metrics, and downstream tasks. The source code is available at https://github.com/ixilai/AMG-Fuse.

</details>


### [49] [Learning Adversarial Augmentation Policies for Robust Garlic Seedling Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26828)
*Soeun Lee,Chanho Kim,Yeji Kang,YoungKi Hong,Byeongkeun Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对户外复杂光照下幼苗检测鲁棒性不足的问题，提出基于对抗增强的轻量级检测框架，在真实田间数据集上验证有效性，无需额外推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幼苗检测方法多依赖稳定成像环境（如无人机/温室），缺乏对地面户外强空间异质光照的适应性；现有光照鲁棒方法常引入额外模块增加计算开销且未针对幼苗检测下游任务优化。

Method: 构建真实户外蒜苗数据集，提出联合优化随机增强策略代理与目标检测器的对抗增强框架，通过结构惩罚约束失真并强化挑战性样本生成。

Result: 检测精度AP50达91.6%（超基线0.9pp/前最优0.2pp）；下游缺失定位精度75.0%/F1=67.0%（较基线+4.8pp/+2.0pp），无额外推理开销。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决复杂光照下幼苗检测难题，实现高精度且计算高效的农业监测方案，适用于实际田间部署。

Abstract: Accurate seedling detection during early growth stages is essential for timely replanting and effective crop management in precision agriculture. However, existing studies are mostly evaluated under relatively stable imaging conditions, such as UAV imagery or greenhouse environments, leaving robust detection under severe and spatially heterogeneous illumination in ground-based outdoor monitoring insufficiently explored. In addition, many illumination-robust detection methods rely on additional enhancement or feature-extraction modules, which increase inference-time overhead and are not tailored to seedling detection and downstream missing seedling localization. To address these gaps, we construct a new garlic seedling dataset captured using a ground-based monitoring platform under real outdoor field conditions with highly variable illumination. We further propose an illumination-robust seedling detection framework based on adversarial augmentation policy learning. The proposed method jointly optimizes a stochastic augmentation policy agent and an object detector, enabling the detector to learn robust representations under challenging visual conditions. A structural penalty is introduced to prevent unrealistic distortions while encouraging challenging augmentations during training. Extensive experiments show that the proposed approach achieves an AP$_{50}$ of 91.6%, improving the baseline by 0.9 percentage points and outperforming the previous best-performing method by 0.2 percentage points. For downstream missing seedling localization, it achieves 75.0% precision and a 67.0% F1-score, improving the baseline by 4.8 and 2.0 percentage points, respectively. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework for practical ground-based agricultural monitoring under complex outdoor lighting conditions without additional inference-time computational overhead.

</details>


### [50] [Identifying the Unknown: Prompt-Free Open Vocabulary Anomaly Recognition for Robot-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26829)
*Philipp Allgeuer,Jan-Gerrit Habekost,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AnomNOVIC框架，结合异常检测MAE和开放词汇分类器NOVIC，实现无需提示的高效开放词汇物体检测，在多个数据集上显著超越现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人系统向开放世界自主性发展过程中，需要能够识别先前未见过的物体，对无需提示且高效的开放词汇物体检测器需求日益增长，但尚未得到充分满足。

Method: 提出两阶段已知工作空间框架AnomNOVIC，结合用于异常检测的掩码自编码器(MAE)与NOVIC开放词汇图像分类器。MAE生成通用对象无关边界框，使NOVIC能够分类显著图像区域而无需预定义候选类列表。

Result: 在桌面机器人环境中达到47.1% AP/57.5% AP50（无提示），提供候选类时达到59.0% AP/72.5% AP50；在野外测试集（48个独特物体）上达到82.6%无提示检测分类准确率；显著超越YOLO-World-v2、OWLv2、YOLOE等基线方法。

Conclusion: AnomNOVIC通过结合异常检测和开放词汇分类，成功实现了高效、无提示的开放词汇物体检测，为机器人开放世界自主性提供了重要的技术突破。

Abstract: Robots operating in real-world environments must in general be able to recognize previously unseen objects. As robotic systems move toward open-world autonomy, there is a growing, yet largely unmet, need for open vocabulary object detectors that are prompt-free and efficient enough for continuous deployment. We present AnomNOVIC, a two-stage known-workspace framework that combines a masked autoencoder (MAE) trained for anomaly detection, with NOVIC, a powerful real-time prompt-free open vocabulary image classifier. The MAE produces generic object-agnostic bounding boxes, allowing NOVIC to classify salient image regions without requiring a predefined candidate class list. We evaluate AnomNOVIC against strong open vocabulary baselines in a tabletop robot-object environment featuring the NICOL humanoid robot, reaching 47.1% AP / 57.5% AP50 for prompt-free recognition, and 59.0% AP / 72.5% AP50 if class candidates are provided. Across additional datasets, including an in-the-wild test set with 48 unique objects, AnomNOVIC reaches up to 82.6% prompt-free detection and classification accuracy. These results significantly surpass all tested open vocabulary baselines, including YOLO-World-v2, OWLv2, and YOLOE.

</details>


### [51] [Liquid Fusion of Heterogeneous Representations Towards General Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26849)
*Ke Chen,Ling Zhou,Guangqi Jiang,Gengshen Wu,Yi Liu,Shoukun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出LFNet，通过融合CNN和SSM的互补频谱特性，结合动态门控机制与Saliency-Guided Upsampling算子，在五种显著性检测任务中实现SOTA性能，兼顾精度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显著性目标检测方法忽视CNN与SSM的固有频谱偏差差异，导致未能充分利用两者在频率偏好上的互补性，限制了模型性能提升。

Method: 提出Liquid Fusion Network (LFNet)，采用动态门控机制融合VMamba（SSM）和ConvNeXt（CNN）特征，结合Saliency-Guided Upsampling算子实现频谱-空间协同设计，支持多模态扩展。

Result: 在RGB、RGB-D、RGB-T、VSOD和VDT五个任务上达到SOTA性能，验证了模型在检测精度与计算效率间的优越平衡。

Conclusion: LFNet通过跨架构频谱融合与动态特征聚合机制，有效解决了多模态显著性检测中的频谱偏差问题，为通用SOD提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: General Salient Object Detection (SOD) aims to identify and segment visually interesting objects from uni-modality or multi-modality scenes, recently advanced by cutting-edge State Space Models (SSMs). However, a critical limitation of current approaches is their neglect of the inherent spectral biases exhibited by different neural network paradigms. By digging to the dataset-level spectral analysis of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and SSMs, their semantic representations are inherently complementary based on their complementary frequency preferences. Inspired by this, we harmonize heterogeneous representations from SSMs and CNNs to bridge their spectral biases for general salient object detection. To this end, inspired by the dynamic information propagation of Liquid Neural Networks (LNNs), we introduce a liquid fusion to dynamically integrates features from two backbones, including VMamba and ConvNeXt, referred to Liquid Fusion Network (LFNet). Concretely, by treating the continuous VMamba features and ConvNeXt features as evolving states and exogenous stimulus, respectively, LFNet employs a dynamic gating mechanism for content-aware feature aggregation. Crucially, this state-stimulus paradigm enables to scale to multi-modal cues, resulting in flexibility in general SOD. Besides, a Saliency-Guided Upsampling (SGU) operator to propagate the features to the shallow layer, which leverages a spectral-spatial co-design to suppress upsampling artifacts while preserving semantics. Extensive experiments across five diverse tasks (RGB, RGB-D, RGB-T, VSOD, and VDT) demonstrate that LFNet achieves state-of-the-art performance, offering a superior trade-off between detection accuracy and model efficiency. Code has been released at https://github.com/cke520/LFNet.

</details>


### [52] [Rolling Shutter Relative Pose Estimation Made Practical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26863)
*Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于仿射对应关系（ACs）的卷帘快门（RS）相机相对位姿估计方法，仅需7个ACs即可实现高效求解，显著降低计算复杂度，并在多个基准测试中达到最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RS相对位姿估计方法需至少20个点匹配，导致RANSAC迭代次数呈指数增长，计算成本过高，难以实际应用。

Method: 引入RS校正的仿射约束，将点扰动与行相关本质矩阵耦合建模，每对AC提供两个独立方程；通过线性化约束、零空间投影消除12个RS未知量，最终用作用矩阵求解20次方程系统。

Result: 在TUM RS基准测试中取得最佳位姿和RS参数精度，并首次实现准确的平移速度估计；在EuRoC MAV数据集上达到与标准5点算法相当的精度，证明其全局快门场景泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法通过仿射约束突破传统点匹配瓶颈，实现RS相机相对位姿估计的实用化，同时提升计算效率与鲁棒性，为动态场景视觉定位提供新解决方案。

Abstract: Rolling shutter (RS) cameras equip virtually all consumer devices, yet RS-aware relative pose estimation has remained impractical: the state-of-the-art solver requires a minimum of 20 point correspondences, making RANSAC-based robust estimation prohibitively expensive due to the exponential dependence of the iteration count on the sample size. We make RS relative pose estimation practical by introducing affine correspondences (ACs) into the RS two-view geometry. We derive novel \emph{RS-corrected affine constraints} that account for the coupling between point perturbations and the row-dependent essential matrix, providing two equations per correspondence beyond the standard epipolar constraint. Building on these constraints, we develop a linearized algebraic solver that estimates pose and RS motion from only 7 ACs. The solver exploits the physical smallness of RS parameters to linearize the constraints, eliminates the 12 RS unknowns via null-space projection, and solves the remaining degree-20 system via action matrices in 1.2\,ms. On the TUM RS benchmark, our method achieves the best pose and RS parameter accuracy among all tested methods and, uniquely among RS solvers, provides accurate translational velocity estimates -- which are poorly conditioned from point correspondences alone due to a $\vec{v}$-$\vec{t}$ coupling. On the global-shutter EuRoC MAV dataset, the solver achieves comparable accuracy to the standard 5-point algorithm, demonstrating that it generalizes well to the GS setting. Code is at https://github.com/danini/rolling_shutter_made_practical.

</details>


### [53] [SpatialFlow-GRPO: Where Spatial Credit Drives Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26872)
*Yankai Yang,Yancheng Long,Wei Chen,Xingyu Lu,Hongyang Wei,Bin Wen,Fan Yang,Tingting Gao,Han Li,Shuo Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SpatialFlow-GRPO框架解决图像编辑中空间均匀性奖励的局限，通过区域级奖励反馈提升细粒度编辑能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Flow-GRPO方法依赖全局图像奖励，无法区分不同空间区域对图像质量的贡献，导致细粒度编辑优化困难。

Method: 设计SpatialFlow-GRPO训练框架，将区域感知奖励转化为语义区域级优化信号，并在策略更新时对齐区域优势与潜在位置；同时训练区域感知奖励模型SFReward，构建SFReward-14K数据集及MultiEditBench评测基准。

Result: 在OmniGen2和FLUX.2-klein-4B模型上，SpatialFlow-GRPO于GEdit-Bench、ImgEdit-Bench和MultiEditBench均优于Flow-GRPO，验证了局部反馈向空间对齐更新信号转化的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入空间细粒度奖励机制，SpatialFlow-GRPO突破了传统全局奖励限制，显著提升了多区域图像编辑的精准度与质量。

Abstract: Recent online reinforcement learning has substantially improved image editing quality. However, existing Flow-GRPO-style methods usually rely on a single whole-image reward, which makes fine-grained editing optimization difficult. We observe that a key obstacle in image editing is this spatial uniformity assumption: a whole-image reward cannot distinguish how different spatial regions contribute to image quality. To address this issue, we propose SpatialFlow-GRPO, a training framework that introduces spatially fine-grained reward feedback. The framework converts region-aware rewards into semantic-region-level optimization signals and aligns region advantages with the corresponding latent positions during policy updates. We also train a region-aware reward model, SFReward, construct SFReward-14K with region-annotated editing samples, and introduce MultiEditBench to evaluate multi-region editing ability. On OmniGen2 and FLUX.2-klein-4B, SpatialFlow-GRPO outperforms Flow-GRPO on GEdit-Bench, ImgEdit-Bench, and MultiEditBench. The results show that SpatialFlow-GRPO converts local feedback into spatially aligned update signals and improves editing quality.

</details>


### [54] [RIS-Assisted Proactive Handover for Reliable mmWave Wireless Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26885)
*Alaa Adnan,Mohammad Al-Quraan,Ahmed Zoha,M. Majid Butt,Sami Muhaidat,Muhammad Ali Imran,Marco Di Renzo,Lina Mohjazi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种RIS辅助的主动切换方法，通过优化RIS元素数量，在满足切换时间约束下平衡信号处理复杂度与链路质量，实现能效提升。实验表明减少12%的RIS元素可降低10%能耗，同时保持信噪比，并在阻挡区域提升15-30dB信号强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 毫米波网络易受视距阻挡影响，现有视觉辅助无线通信（VAWC）在无邻近基站时难以实现主动切换（PHO），而传统RIS方案因大量元素导致配置延迟，无法满足时间敏感场景需求。

Method: 设计基于粒子群优化（PSO）的离线优化框架，动态调整RIS元素分配策略，在满足端到端切换时延约束的前提下，最小化信号处理复杂度并保障链路质量。

Result: 优化方案使RIS元素数量减少12%时能耗降低10%，信噪比无损失；在阻挡区域实现15-30dB信号增益，且切换时机准确率达100%。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了RIS辅助切换中的时延-能效矛盾，为毫米波网络在复杂遮挡环境下的可靠通信提供了新范式。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave (mmWave) networks are highly susceptible to line-of-sight (LoS) blockages. Vision-aided wireless communications (VAWC) enable proactive handovers (PHO) to mitigate such blockages; however, PHO becomes challenging when no nearby base station (BS) is available. In such cases, reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) can be used to restore connectivity. To ensure timely PHO, the RIS configuration time must be taken into account, as the large number of RIS elements can limit responsiveness in time-sensitive scenarios. This work proposes a novel RIS-assisted PHO approach that optimizes the number of allocated RIS elements to balance signal processing complexity and link quality under handover timing constraints, making the RIS-assisted link more energy-efficient. An optimization problem based on particle swarm optimization (PSO) is formulated to determine the optimal end-to-end RIS link setup that runs offline to bypass latency constraints. Results show that reducing the number of RIS elements by 12\% leads to a 10\% decrease in dissipated energy without compromising the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Moreover, the RIS-assisted link achieves a 15--30 dB improvement in blocked regions while maintaining accurate PHO timing.

</details>


### [55] [Bridging Vision and Language Concepts through Optimal Transport Semantic Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26891)
*Chenyang Zhang,Anqi Dong,Guangming Zhu,Nuoye Xiong,Siyuan Wang,Lin Mei,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 现有概念瓶颈模型依赖预对齐编码器或全局相似度，难以实现细粒度概念定位和真实语义几何建模。本文提出OTF-CBM，通过动态跨模态传输和逆最优传输学习语义成本，结合不平衡最优传输流匹配与速度基激活机制，在分类准确性和概念忠实度上显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言CBM依赖预对齐编码器或全局余弦相似度，导致细粒度概念定位能力不足且无法准确反映跨模态语义几何关系，亟需动态化对齐方法。

Method: 提出OTF-CBM框架：1) 通过逆最优传输学习数据驱动的语义成本矩阵；2) 采用不平衡最优传输流匹配建模视觉补丁与文本概念的动态语义转换；3) 利用速度基概念激活机制避免ODE积分计算。

Result: 实验表明OTF-CBM在分类准确率和概念忠实度指标上均优于现有方法，同时保持可解释的几何关系建模能力。

Conclusion: 首次将最优传输理论引入CBM概念对齐过程，为跨模态可解释推理提供了新的几何动力学范式，验证了动态语义建模的有效性。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) promise transparent reasoning by predicting through human-interpretable concepts, yet their effectiveness fundamentally depends on how well visual and textual representations are aligned or matched. Existing vision-language CBMs often rely on pre-aligned encoders or global cosine similarity, which obscures fine-grained concept localization and fails to reflect true semantic geometry. In this work, we rethink concept alignment as a dynamic cross-modal transport process instead of static projection and propose the Optimal Transport Flow Concept Bottleneck Model (OTF-CBM). It first learns a data-driven semantic cost via Inverse Optimal Transport to measure cross-modal distances, and then performs unbalanced optimal-transport-based flow matching to model semantic transitions between visual patches and textual concepts. With velocity-based concept activation, OTF-CBM captures interpretable geometric relations without ODE integration. Experiments further show that OTF-CBM achieves superior classification accuracy and concept faithfulness, offering a new geometric and dynamical perspective for interpretable cross-modal reasoning.

</details>


### [56] [Modeling Local, Global, and Cross-Modal Context in Multimodal 3D MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26894)
*Minh Duc Do,Tillmann Rheude,Noel Kronenberg,Roland Eils,Benjamin Wild*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MICViT模型，通过结合四种注意力机制显式建模多模态脑MRI数据中的模态内和跨模态交互，在三个数据集上验证其优于现有方法，尤其在多模态输入下性能提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑MRI分析面临高维3D多模态数据整合挑战，现有方法难以有效融合互补信息，且神经影像研究样本量有限，制约模型泛化能力。

Method: 设计MICViT架构，融合四种注意力机制：模态内局部/全局注意力学习单模态特征，跨模态局部/全局注意力捕获模态间交互。

Result: 在UK Biobank、SOOP、Cam-CAN三个数据集的脑龄预测任务中，MICViT超越CNN和Transformer基线，且多模态输入增益随模态数量增加而显著提升。

Conclusion: 显式建模模态内/跨模态交互是释放多模态脑MRI潜力的关键，为神经影像表征学习提供新方向。

Abstract: Brain MRI poses a fundamental challenge for machine learning: models must learn from high-dimensional 3D data spanning multiple co-registered modalities, despite the limited sample sizes typical of neuroimaging studies relative to the diversity in anatomy, pathology, and acquisition conditions. While multimodal imaging provides complementary information critical for clinical interpretation, effectively integrating these signals remains difficult. We propose Multimodal Intra- and Cross-Context Vision Transformer (MICViT), a 3D vision transformer that explicitly models both modality-specific representations and cross-modal interactions across local and global contexts. Concretely, MICViT combines four attention mechanisms: modality-specific local and global attention for intra-modal feature learning, and cross-modal local and global attention to capture interactions between modalities. We evaluate MICViT on brain age prediction across three heterogeneous datasets (UK Biobank, n=41,404; SOOP, n=1,062; Cam-CAN, n=613) using multiple MRI modalities (e.g. T1, FLAIR, DWI, SWI). MICViT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art CNN and transformer baselines in 3D settings. Notably, it benefits more strongly from multimodal inputs, yielding larger performance gains as additional modalities are incorporated. These results demonstrate that explicitly modeling intra- and cross-modal interactions is key to unlocking the full potential of multimodal brain MRI, highlighting a promising direction for representation learning in neuroimaging.

</details>


### [57] [FlameVQA: A Physically-Grounded UAV Wildfire VQA Benchmark with Radiometric Thermal Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27128)
*Mobin Habibpour,John Spodnik,Niloufar Alipour Talemi,Fatemeh Afghah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FlameVQA，一个用于无人机野火智能的多选视觉问答基准，结合RGB和热成像数据，评估了MLLMs在野火监测任务中的表现，发现其在跨模态推理上有潜力但在特定场景下仍需改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机野火监测面临烟雾、尺度变化和遮挡等挑战，仅靠RGB图像难以进行可靠的复杂场景推理，需要更鲁棒的多模态理解方法。

Method: 构建了FlameVQA基准，基于FLAME 3数据集，利用RGB和热成像TIFF文件，包含34个多选题覆盖6类任务。采用MLLM辅助标注结合热规则和人工审核确保标签质量，并评估了现有MLLMs的性能。

Result: 现有MLLMs在有明确跨模态线索时表现强劲，但在浓烟下的火情检测和覆盖范围估算上存在显著不足。

Conclusion: 当前通用MLLMs需要针对灾害监测领域进行特定适应和优化，才能更好地支持野火监测任务。相关资源已开源。

Abstract: Wildfire monitoring from UAVs requires reliable reasoning over complex aerial scenes, where smoke, scale variation, and occlusions often limit RGB-only interpretation. We introduce FlameVQA, a multiple-choice visual question answering benchmark for UAV-based wildfire intelligence built on FLAME 3, leveraging paired RGB imagery and radiometric thermal TIFFs for temperature-grounded, safety-critical reasoning. FlameVQA includes 34 multiple-choice questions per image spanning six operational capability groups, covering tasks such as detection, localization, distribution/coverage estimation, cross-modal reasoning, and flight planning. To ensure label reliability, we combine MLLM-assisted annotation with deterministic thermal rules and cross-question consistency checks, followed by human auditing. We also evaluate representative MLLMs on FlameVQA to provide baselines for future work. Results show strong performance when explicit cross-modal cues are available, but notable failures on presence detection under heavy smoke and on coverage estimation. These findings suggest that current MLLMs require domain-specific adaptation to better support disaster and wildfire monitoring. The dataset and benchmark code are open-source at github.com/mobiiin/WildFire_VQA

</details>


### [58] [Tractography-Driven Synthetic Data Generation for Fiber Bundle Segmentation in Tracer Histology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26898)
*Kyriaki-Margarita Bintsi,Sparsh Makharia,Yaël Balbastre,Joselyn Romero Avila,Julia F. Lehman,Suzanne N. Haber,Anastasia Yendiki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种合成数据增强的纤维束分割框架，使用dMRI作为生成先验合成训练数据，在猕猴示踪剂组织学上实现了与最先进水平相当的性能，但仅需1/3的手动注释数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散磁共振成像(dMRI)纤维束成像虽能非侵入性重建白质通路，但受限于间接、低分辨率测量。非人灵长类示踪剂研究是验证dMRI的金标准，但需要耗时的手动纤维束注释。

Method: 使用离体dMRI纤维束成像作为生成先验合成2D图像补丁，提供真实前景纹理，与块面照片背景组合并通过域随机化多样化。使用2D U-Net在混合真实和合成补丁上训练。

Result: 在保留大脑上实验显示，相比仅用真实数据训练，该方法在不同大脑和纤维束密度下泛化性能更好。仅用合成数据训练性能差，强调真实监督的必要性。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了与最先进水平相当的性能，同时仅需3倍少的手动注释数据，为dMRI验证提供了高效的自动化工具。

Abstract: Diffusion MRI (dMRI) tractography enables non-invasive reconstruction of white-matter pathways, but its accuracy is fundamentally limited by indirect, low-resolution measurements of axonal organization. Tracer injection studies in non-human primates provide a gold standard for validating dMRI tractography. This, however, requires time-consuming manual annotation of fiber bundles in histology sections. We propose a synthetic-data augmented framework for automated fiber bundle segmentation in macaque tracer histology. Our approach uses ex vivo dMRI tractography as a generative prior to synthesize 2D image patches for training. This provides us with sufficiently realistic foreground texture, which we compose with backgrounds from blockface photos and diversify via domain randomization. A 2D U-Net is trained on mixed real and synthetic patches. Experiments on held-out brains demonstrate improved generalization across brains and fiber bundle densities compared to training with real data only. Training with synthetic data only leads to poor performance, underscoring the need for real supervision. Overall, our approach achieves performance comparable to the state-of-the-art while requiring 3x less manually annotated data.

</details>


### [59] [OctoSense: Self-Supervised Learning for Multimodal Robot Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27317)
*Anthony Bisulco,Jeremy Wang,Kostas Daniilidis,Randall Balestriero,Pratik Chaudhari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OctoSense开源多模态传感器平台及数据集，并基于此开发了一种"晚期融合"掩码自编码器方法，实现了对异构传感器数据的高效自监督学习，在多个视觉任务上超越现有图像基础模型，且在夜间或传感器退化场景下表现鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型主要依赖单一图像模态，难以有效处理机器人系统中多传感器数据异构性（不同表示、频率、延迟和噪声）的问题，尤其在传感器退化或夜间等挑战性条件下性能显著下降。需要一种能够统一处理多模态异步数据并适应真实世界复杂环境的方法。

Method: 设计了一种"晚期融合"掩码自编码器架构：(i) 使用模态特定的分词器处理不同传感器的时空特性；(ii) 在推理时缓存模态特定的token以处理新到来的测量数据。该方法在OctoSense数据集上进行多模态自监督学习训练。

Result: (1) 计算效率高：在NVIDIA 5090和Orin NX上分别仅需6.68ms和112ms计算表示；(2) 性能优越：在光流、深度估计、语义分割和自身运动估计（平移、旋转、转向角）等任务上优于现有图像基础模型；(3) 鲁棒性强：在夜间或传感器数据退化情况下仍能保持可靠的预测性能。

Conclusion: OctoSense平台和数据集为多模态机器人感知提供了重要基础，所提出的晚期融合掩码自编码器方法成功解决了异构传感器数据的统一表示学习问题，在真实世界复杂场景下展现出优越的性能和实用性，推动了多模态自监督学习在机器人领域的应用。

Abstract: We present OctoSense, an open-source sensor platform with stereo RGB and event cameras, LiDAR, a thermal camera, an inertial measurement unit, RTK-corrected global positioning system, and proprioception (CAN bus data from a car, and joint angles for a quadruped robot). The eponymous OctoSense dataset contains 59 hours of time-synchronized driving data across different types of environments at different times of the day, including situations with highly degraded sensors. We demonstrate multi-modal self-supervised learning using such real-world robotics data, where sensors have different representations, frequencies, latencies and noise. Our approach, a "late-fusion" masked autoencoder, (i) uses modality-specific tokenizers to account for different spatiotemporal characteristics of these sensors, and (ii) caches modality-specific tokens at inference time to process new measurements as they come. This architecture (i) is fast (6.68 ms and 112 ms on NVIDIA 5090 and Orin NX respectively, to compute the representation), (ii) performs better than existing image-only foundation models on tasks such as estimation of optical flow, depth, semantic segmentation, and ego-motion (translation, rotation, and steering angle), and (iii) predicts robustly at nighttime or in situations where sensory data is degraded. See our project page for links to the dataset, code, and supplementary videos: https://abisulco.com/octosense/.

</details>


### [60] [Confidence-Aware Tool Orchestration for Robust Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26904)
*Yangfan He,Yujin Choi,Jaehong Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Robust-TO框架通过显式整合帧级可信度评估机制，解决了视频推理模型在真实扰动下的盲目信任问题，在保持高效的同时显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频推理模型隐含假设所有输入帧等可靠，导致在运动模糊、眩光或遮挡等现实干扰下出现15-30%准确率下降且无法感知证据退化，即"盲目信任问题"。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：1) 异构视觉工具统一接口处理子查询与可信帧集；2) 输出含预测结果、时间定位和校准可靠性评分的证据；3) 通过三层合成过程(高/中/低置信度)加权证据，并设计置信度-成本GRPO奖励联合优化正确性、可靠性与效率。

Result: 在8项任务的视频推理基准测试中：干净输入达56.4%准确率(超最强开源基线10.6%，优于Gemini-2.5-Pro的46.2%)；5类现实干扰下保持54.3%准确率(超基线5.8%)，且准确率下降幅度最小。

Conclusion: Robust-TO首次将帧级可信度评估深度融入视频推理全流程，在真实场景扰动下实现显著鲁棒性提升，为具身智能系统的可靠视觉理解提供新范式。

Abstract: Video reasoning language models implicitly assume that every input frame is equally reliable. This leads to what we term the Blind Trust Problem: under realistic perturbations such as motion blur, glare, or occlusion, frontier video reasoning models can suffer 15-30%p accuracy drops on real-world embodied benchmarks, while remaining unaware that their visual evidence has been degraded. To address this challenge, we propose Robust-TO, an agentic video understanding framework that explicitly integrates per-frame trustworthiness into every stage of reasoning. Robust-TO organizes heterogeneous visual perception tools under a unified evidence interface. Each tool receives a sub-query derived from the original question and a set of trustworthy frames selected by the reliability-relevance score. It returns evidence in a shared format: a concrete prediction (e.g., a bounding box, motion trajectory, recognized text, or action label), temporal grounding, and a calibrated reliability score. During reasoning, these calibrated scores guide evidence weighting in a three-tier synthesis process (high/medium/low) and define a confidence-cost GRPO reward that jointly optimizes correctness, evidence reliability, and efficiency. On two video reasoning benchmarks spanning eight tasks, Robust-TO achieves 56.4% average accuracy on clean inputs, surpassing the strongest open-source baseline by 10.6%p and outperforming Gemini-2.5-Pro (46.2%). Under five realistic corruption types, Robust-TO maintains 54.3% average accuracy, 5.8%p above the strongest open-source baseline, while exhibiting the smallest clean-to-corrupted accuracy drop among all compared methods.

</details>


### [61] [Qwen-Image-Agent: Bridging the Context Gap in Real-World Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26907)
*Zekai Zhang,Jiahao Li,Jie Zhang,Kaiyuan Gao,Kun Yan,Lihan Jiang,Ningyuan Tang,Shengming Yin,Tianhe Wu,Xiaoyue Chen,Xiao Xu,Yan Shu,Yanran Zhang,Yixian Xu,Yuxiang Chen,Zhendong Wang,Zihao Liu,Zikai Zhou,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao,Chenfei Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Qwen-Image-Agent框架解决文本到图像模型中的上下文差距问题，通过整合规划、推理、搜索、记忆和反馈机制提升生成效果，并在多个基准测试中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型难以处理现实中的模糊、隐晦或需最新知识的请求，存在用户上下文与生成所需上下文之间的不匹配问题（即上下文差距）。

Method: 设计统一智能体框架Qwen-Image-Agent，包含上下文感知规划（识别缺失上下文并制定获取策略）和上下文接地（通过推理、搜索、记忆及反馈收集上下文），以逐步构建完整生成上下文。

Result: 在IA-Bench、Mindbench和WISE-Verified基准测试中，该方法显著优于现有基线模型，在计划、推理、搜索和记忆四项核心能力上均实现最优表现。

Conclusion: 该研究有效弥合了文本到图像生成中的上下文差距，验证了多模态智能体框架在复杂场景下的优越性，为未来上下文感知生成技术提供了新范式。

Abstract: While text-to-image (T2I) models have achieved remarkable progress, they struggle with real-world requests that are often underspecified, implicit, or dependent on up-to-date knowledge. We identify this challenge as the Context Gap: the mismatch between the user context and the sufficient generation context for T2I models. To bridge this gap, we propose Qwen-Image-Agent, a unified agentic framework that integrates plan, reason, search, memory and feedback in a context-centric manner. Qwen-Image-Agent treats user input as partial context and progressively constructs the generation context through Context-Aware Planning and Context Grounding. Specifically, Context-Aware Planning identifies missing context and plans how it should be acquired and used, while Context Grounding gathers this context from reason, search, memory, and feedback. To evaluate agentic image generation, we further introduce Image Agent Bench (IA-Bench), a benchmark covering four core image agent capabilities: Plan, Reason, Search, and Memory. Experiments on IA-Bench, Mindbench and WISE-Verified show that Qwen-Image-Agent outperforms strong baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [62] [PhysRAG: Enhancing Physics-Awareness in Video Generation via Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26916)
*Kexu Cheng,Zicheng Liu,Mingju Gao,Chunhe Song,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysRAG通过检索增强生成（RAG）与高质量数据筛选，显著提升视频生成模型的物理规律遵循能力，在视觉质量与物理合规性上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型难以准确模拟热力学、力学、光学等复杂物理现象，且缺乏高质量物理数据支持，导致生成结果物理一致性不足。

Method: 提出PhysRAG框架：1) 基于WISA-80K构建两阶段数据过滤管道，筛选7K高质量视频；2) 建立物理视频数据库；3) 设计可学习查询机制，通过RAG将物理知识注入扩散模型。

Result: 在PhyGenBench和VBench基准测试中全面超越现有模型，消融实验验证了数据过滤、RAG机制及物理信息提取模块的有效性。

Conclusion: PhysRAG有效解决了视频生成中的物理感知难题，开源代码与数据集将推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Developing physically aware video generation models remains a significant challenge due to the difficulty in capturing diverse physical phenomena, such as thermal dynamics, mechanics, and optics. In this work, we introduce PhysRAG, a novel pipeline that enhances physical awareness in video generation through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). To address the issue of limited high-quality data, we design a two-stage data filtering pipeline based on the WISA-80K dataset, resulting in a curated set of 7K high-quality videos for training. Furthermore, we construct a physical video database and develop a mechanism to inject physical knowledge into a video diffusion model using learnable queries. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in both visual quality and physical rule compliance, surpassing existing models in benchmarks such as PhyGenBench and VBench. We conduct extensive ablation studies to validate the effectiveness of our key components, including the data filtering pipeline, RAG mechanism, and method for physical information extraction. To facilitate future research, our code, data, and models are prepared for release at https://github.com/sediment1024/PhysRAG.

</details>


### [63] [PortraitGen: Exemplar-Driven GRPO with Dual-Reward Guidance for Photorealistic Portrait Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26930)
*Xiaomin Li,Qian Liang,Yinan Li,Ying Zhang,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Huchuan Lu,Xu Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出PortraitGen框架，通过引入真实图像到GRPO采样组及设计双重奖励机制，显著提升人像生成的真实感并抑制AI伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GRPO的文本到图像后训练方法存在两大局限：1) 后训练缺乏真实图像导致生成边界固化；2) 优化过程缺少针对细粒度AI伪影（如油亮皮肤）的奖励信号，致使生成结果过度追求表面美学而忽视生物合理性。

Method: 提出双创新机制：1) 将真实图像直接嵌入GRPO采样组，通过图像反转技术获取其转移概率与潜在表示以突破生成边界；2) 设计互补双奖励体系——OmniReward保障整体质量，AI-Portrait专项优化人像保真度。同步构建专业人像基准测试集PortraitBench。

Result: 实验表明PortraitGen在抑制AI伪影、提升生物合理性方面显著优于基线方法，在PortraitBench上实现前所未有的照片级真实感。

Conclusion: 通过真实图像注入与细粒度奖励引导，PortraitGen有效解决了现有方法的根本性缺陷，为高保真人像生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning like Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) has significantly advanced text-to-image post-training. However, current methods often favor superficial aesthetics, such as over-saturated colors, leaving critical flaws like AI artifacts and biological implausibilities unresolved. We attribute these limitations to two primary factors: (1) The absence of real images during post-training confines GRPO sampling to the original distribution, failing to break inherent generative boundaries; (2) the optimization process lacks specific rewards targeting fine-grained artifacts like overly oily skin and other AI artifacts. To address this, we propose PortraitGen, a novel framework tailored for photorealistic portrait generation. First, we break inherent generative boundaries by directly introducing real images into the GRPO sampling groups, where image inversion is employed to obtain their transition probabilities and latents. Second, to explicitly steer the model toward photorealism, we introduce a complementary dual-reward mechanism: OmniReward for general quality and AI-Portrait for human-centric fidelity. Furthermore, we curate PortraitBench, a comprehensive portrait-centric benchmark. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PortraitGen significantly outperforms existing baselines, effectively suppressing AI artifacts and achieving unprecedented photorealism.

</details>


### [64] [Focusing on What Matters: Saliency-Harnessing Accurate Routing for Diffusion MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26938)
*Haoyou Deng,Keyu Yan,Chaojie Mao,Xiang Wang,Yu Liu,Changxin Gao,Nong Sang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SharpMoE通过利用干净潜在特征作为无噪声路由信号，并引入轨迹路由损失，解决了扩散MoE中路由器因噪声干扰无法准确分配资源的问题，显著提升视觉生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散MoE框架的路由器因依赖去噪过程中噪声污染的潜在特征，无法准确识别并分配更多计算资源给显著token，导致效率与性能受限。

Method: 提出SharpMoE后训练框架，采用显著性利用的准确路由机制，使用干净潜在特征作为无噪声路由引导；同时设计轨迹路由损失约束多步去噪过程中的计算资源分配。

Result: 实验证明SharpMoE作为即插即用方案可增强预训练MoE模型，在视觉生成任务中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: SharpMoE有效解决扩散MoE的路由分配缺陷，通过去噪干扰和轨迹约束实现精准资源分配，为视觉生成提供高效可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have emerged as a powerful paradigm for scaling diffusion models in visual generation. Recent advancements have focused on adaptively allocating computational resources across diverse tokens to improve efficiency and performance. However, we identify a routing assignment problem in existing diffusion MoE frameworks: the router fails to accurately allocate more computational resources to salient tokens. Our analysis attributes this failure to the router's reliance on noise-corrupted latent features throughout the denoising process. Such stochastic noise obscures the critical structural and textural information, thereby preventing the router from effectively distinguishing salient tokens. To address this, we propose SharpMoE, a post-training framework with a saliency-harnessing accurate routing mechanism, which utilizes clean latent features as a noise-free guidance signal for routing. By bypassing the noise-distorted inputs, SharpMoE provides the router with clear saliency guidance, enabling the identification of salient tokens even in high-noise stages. Furthermore, we introduce a trajectory routing loss to constrain the compute allocation throughout the multi-step denoising trajectory, ensuring precise resource allocation along the generation rollout. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SharpMoE serves as a versatile, plug-and-play solution that further enhances the pretrained, converged MoE models, achieving state-of-the-art performance in visual generation.

</details>


### [65] [TraMP-LLaMA: Generative Interpretability with Decoupled Instruction Tuning for Facial Expression Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26942)
*Shuchao Duan,Alan Whone,Hossein Rahmani,Jun Liu,Majid Mirmehdi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究针对面部表情质量评估（FEQA）方法缺乏可解释性的问题，提出TraMP-LLaMA框架，通过多模态融合与解耦指令微调策略，实现帕金森病评估中严重性评分预测与结构化文本报告生成的统一，扩展PFED5数据集并验证其性能优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FEQA方法仅提供严重性评分，缺乏对面部运动证据的显式解释，限制了模型在帕金森病评估中的可解释性，难以验证预测依据。

Method: 构建TraMP-LLaMA框架，融合RGB外观与关键点轨迹特征，采用解耦指令微调策略降低评分预测与文本生成的任务干扰；扩展PFED5数据集为PFED5-plus，添加专家标注的运动描述文本。

Result: 在PFED5-plus数据集上，该框架在报告生成任务中超越视频-语言基线模型，在联合多表情训练下实现最优严重性预测性能，斯皮尔曼等级相关系数较其他方法提升至少4.39%。

Conclusion: 提出首个统一多模态FEQA框架，通过可解释文本报告增强模型透明度，为帕金森病评估提供更可靠的诊断辅助工具，并开源数据集与代码推动领域发展。

Abstract: Existing facial expression quality assessment (FEQA) methods typically produce only a severity score, without explicitly communicating the observable facial motion evidence that supports the prediction. This limits interpretability and makes it difficult to inspect the basis of model outputs in Parkinson's disease assessment. To address this gap, we propose TraMP-LLaMA, a unified multimodal framework that jointly predicts severity scores and generates structured textual reports from facial motion cues. The framework integrates RGB appearance and landmark trajectory cues, and adopts a decoupled instruction-tuning strategy to reduce task interference between severity prediction and language generation. To support this task, we further extend the PFED5 dataset with expert-guided textual motion descriptions and construct PFED5-plus. Experiments on PFED5-plus show that TraMP-LLaMA outperforms competitive video-language baselines in report generation and achieves the best severity prediction performance among the compared methods under joint multi-expression training, improving Spearman's rank correlation by at least 4.39 percent over all competing methods. The text annotations and code are available at https://github.com/shuchaoduan/TraMP-LLaMA.

</details>


### [66] [Scaling Multi-Reference Image Generation with Dynamic Reward Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26947)
*Wenwang Huang,Yusen Fu,Junjie Wang,Mengfei Huang,Yulin Li,Gan Liu,Jing Cai,Yancheng He,Zhuotao Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究针对多参考图像生成（MRIG）评估不足的问题，提出新基准OmniRef-Bench和两阶段训练框架DyRef，显著提升开源模型在复杂MRIG任务中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准无法充分评估复杂多参考图像生成场景，阻碍该领域发展。

Method: 构建覆盖复杂参考类型组合及大量参考图像的OmniRef-Bench基准；提出DyRef两阶段框架：第一阶段监督微调赋予基础能力，第二阶段引入难度感知优势重加权（DAR）动态调整优化目标，以及判别性奖励缩放（DRS）扩大组内奖励差异以优化策略。

Result: 主流开源模型在复杂MRIG场景表现差且随混合类型参考图数量增加性能显著下降；DyRef在OmniRef-Bench及单图编辑基准上显著提升性能。

Conclusion: OmniRef-Bench有效暴露现有模型局限性，DyRef通过创新优化策略显著增强模型复杂MRIG能力并展现良好泛化性。

Abstract: While personalized image generation has achieved remarkable progress, multi-reference image generation (MRIG) remains a challenging task. Most existing benchmarks fail to adequately evaluate complex MRIG scenarios, hindering further progress in this area. To better assess model performance on complex MRIG tasks, we introduce OmniRef-Bench, a benchmark that covers complex combinations of reference image types and a large number of reference images. Evaluations on OmniRef-Bench show that mainstream open-source models struggle in complex MRIG scenarios, and their performance deteriorates significantly as the number of mixed-type reference images increases. To address this issue, we propose DyRef, a two-stage training framework. In the first stage, supervised fine-tuning equips the model with the basic capability to handle complex MRIG tasks. In the second stage, we introduce Difficulty-aware Advantage Reweighting (DAR) and Discriminative Reward Scaling (DRS). DAR dynamically adjusts the optimization objective to improve performance when handling a large number of mixed-type reference images. DRS enlarges intra-group reward differences for more effective policy optimization. Experiments demonstrate that DyRef significantly improves the performance of open-source models on OmniRef-Bench and single-image editing benchmarks, demonstrating the effectiveness and generalization capability of our approach.

</details>


### [67] [Computer Vision for MOBA Analytics: A Dataset and Baseline for Visibility Analysis in Dota 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26970)
*Ricardo da Rocha Carvalho,Eloísa Oliveira,Luiz Bernardo Martins Kummer,Emerson Cabrera Paraiso,Rayson Laroca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出Dota2-Vis数据集和可见性分析基线流程，通过视频分析解决传统MOBA分析无法捕获实际可见信息的问题，使用YOLO11l检测小地图玩家图标，揭示传统结构化数据难以发现的行为模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOBA分析研究主要依赖结构化数据，无法直接捕获比赛中各队实际可见的信息，存在分析局限性。

Method: 构建Dota2-Vis数据集（144场TI2025比赛，288个全高清视频，2477张手动标注小地图图像），评估多种现代目标检测器进行玩家图标检测，使用最佳模型估计对手可见玩家存在。

Result: YOLO11l表现最佳，在密集杂乱小地图场景中可靠识别玩家图标。生成的可见性曲线揭示了玩家、英雄、角色和团队层面的行为模式，补充传统MOBA分析。

Conclusion: 视频可见性分析方法能够有效揭示传统结构化数据难以获得的行为差异，为MOBA分析提供新的视角。数据集和代码已公开共享。

Abstract: Introduction: Most Multiplayer Online Battle Arena (MOBA) analytics studies rely on structured data, which does not directly capture what each team could actually see during a match. Objective: This work introduces Dota2-Vis, a video-based dataset, and a baseline pipeline for visibility analysis in professional Dota 2 matches. Methodology: The dataset comprises all 144 matches from The International 2025, recorded from both team perspectives, totaling 288 Full HD videos, together with 2,477 manually annotated minimap images. We evaluate multiple variants of a modern object detector for player-icon detection and use the best-performing model to estimate opponent-visible player presence over time. Results: YOLO11l (large) achieved the best overall performance, reliably identifying player icons even in dense and visually cluttered minimap scenes. The resulting visibility curves reveal player, hero, role, and team-level patterns that complement conventional MOBA analytics, highlighting behavioral differences that are difficult to obtain from structured data alone. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/RicardoRCarvalho/dota2-vis/.

</details>


### [68] [Geometric Gradient Rectification for Safe Open-Set Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26973)
*Jiahe Chen,Qian Shao,Qiyuan Chen,Jiaying He,Jintai Chen,Jian Wu,Hongxia Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于开放集半监督学习的新方法，通过梯度层面的控制来解决现有方法在过滤样本和利用伪标签之间的权衡问题。该方法在CIFAR和ImageNet基准上取得了更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放集半监督学习需要利用可能包含分布外异常值的未标记数据，同时保持对分布内类别的性能。现有方法要么过滤可疑样本（可能误删有用样本），要么利用伪标签但引入与监督学习冲突的梯度。因此，作者希望从样本选择转向梯度层面的控制。

Method: 提出几何梯度修正（GGR）框架，以监督梯度为锚点，将冲突的辅助梯度投影到梯度空间中的可行区域，确保辅助更新在修正后的坐标块内一阶非对抗，同时保留可能携带有用表示信号的正交分量。进一步扩展为子空间感知修正以稳定锚点。

Result: 在CIFAR和ImageNet基准上的实验表明，GGR在大多数设置下改进了代表性OSSL基线，并在封闭集泛化和开放集鲁棒性方面均取得提升。

Conclusion: GGR方法有效解决了开放集半监督学习中的梯度冲突问题，提升了模型性能，代码将开源。

Abstract: Open-set semi-supervised learning aims to leverage unlabeled data that may contain out-of-distribution outliers while maintaining performance on in-distribution classes. Existing methods mainly follow two paradigms: filtering suspicious samples or incorporating unlabeled objectives with soft weighting. We argue that both face a common trade-off: aggressive filtering can discard informative but hard ID samples, whereas utilization can introduce auxiliary gradients that conflict with supervised learning when pseudo labels are wrong. We therefore shift the focus from sample selection to gradient-level control. We propose \textit{Geometric Gradient Rectification} (GGR), a plug-in framework that uses the supervised gradient as an anchor and projects conflicting auxiliary gradients onto an admissible region in gradient space. This makes the applied auxiliary update first-order non-opposing within the rectified coordinate block while preserving orthogonal components that may still carry useful representation signals. We further extend GGR with subspace-aware rectification to stabilize the anchor under noisy mini-batch gradients. Experiments on CIFAR and ImageNet benchmarks show that GGR improves representative OSSL baselines in most settings and yields gains in both closed-set generalization and open-set robustness. Code will be available at https://github.com/JiaheChen2002/GGR.

</details>


### [69] [Unison: Benchmarking Unified Multimodal Models via Synergistic Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26984)
*Jinyu Liu,Xincheng Shuai,Henghui Ding,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Unison是一个针对统一多模态模型的综合基准，包含2169个高质量样本，通过评估理解与生成的协同效应揭示现有模型局限，并提供诊断性评估框架和人类对齐的评估模型Unison-Judge。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型评估通常将理解与生成能力割裂，忽视了两者协同作用的重要性，导致无法全面衡量模型的实际性能。

Method: 构建Unison基准，包含内部一致性、理解引导生成、生成引导理解及相互增强四个维度；设计统一/解耦双轨评估机制；开发Unison-Judge评估模型确保人类对齐性。

Result: 系统评估显示当前统一多模态系统在协同能力上存在显著不足，如理解与生成模块间缺乏有效交互，验证了统一建模的潜在增益空间。

Conclusion: Unison为统一多模态模型提供了首个全面评估框架，其开源资源将推动领域发展，未来研究需聚焦跨模态协同机制优化。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models capable of both understanding and generation have achieved remarkable strides. However, despite their unified designs, existing evaluations typically assess understanding and generation capabilities in isolation, overlooking the synergy between comprehension and generation. To bridge this gap, we introduce Unison, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 2,169 high-quality unified task samples, designed to evaluate joint understanding and generation in unified multimodal models. Unison offers three key strengths: 1) Comprehensive Dimensions: Unison encompasses internal consistency, understanding-guided generation, generation-guided understanding, and mutual enhancement to enable holistic evaluation. 2) Diagnostic Evaluation: it provides both unified and decoupled tracks for understanding and generation, allowing fine-grained attribution of failure modes and quantitative analysis of the gains from unified modeling. 3) Human Alignment: we also introduce Unison-Judge, an evaluation model well aligned with human judgments to ensure reliable assessment. Based on systematic evaluations of state-of-the-art models on Unison, we uncover critical limitations in current unified multimodal systems and highlight promising directions for future research. Codes, Unison and Unison-Judge are publicly available at https://github.com/FudanCVL/Unison.

</details>


### [70] [Event-Aware Instructed Assistant for Referring Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26994)
*Jinyu Liu,Henghui Ding,Shuting He,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对现有指代视频分割方法将视频视为单一事件导致混淆的问题，提出EVIS模型通过可学习事件查询分解视频为简单事件，结合文本引导的层次化理解和对象-像素混合学习实现精准分割，在5个基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指代视频分割方法通常将视频视为单一事件，忽视了视频包含多个独立事件的事实，导致模型难以处理复杂内容并易产生混淆与幻觉。

Method: 提出EVIS（事件感知视频指令分割助手），利用文本引导的可学习事件查询将视频分解为简单事件，提取事件感知视觉-文本特征实现层次化理解；同时引入对象-像素混合学习机制，融合细粒度像素特征与对象先验查询以支持长期目标跟踪。

Result: 在5个公开基准数据集上的实验表明，EVIS在指代视频分割任务中展现出显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过事件分解与混合学习策略，有效解决了传统方法对复杂视频内容理解不足的问题，实现了更精准的指代视频分割。

Abstract: Existing referring video segmentation methods often treat a video as a single event consisting of multiple images, overlooking the fact that a video typically contains multiple distinct events. Under such a mechanism, the model needs to directly understand all the complex content in the video and text, which can easily lead to confusion and hallucinations. To address this issue, we propose to decompose a video to a set of simple events by learnable Event Query, and understand complex video content in an event-by-event, easy-to-understand manner. This is based on the observation that natural language expressions often divide a video into distinct, text-related segments, each representing a separate event within a compound event. We introduce EVIS, an Event-Aware Video Instructed Segmentation Assistant, which utilizes text-guided Event Queries to partition a video into simple events, extracting event-aware visual-text features to achieve a hierarchical understanding of the video. Additionally, we propose Object-Pixel-Hybrid Learning, which enables the MLLMs to track targets in long-term videos by integrating fine-grained pixel features with prior object queries. Extensive experimental results on 5 public benchmarks demonstrate EVIS's strong performance in addressing the referring video segmentation task.

</details>


### [71] [On-board Remote-Sensing Foundation Models for Unsupervised Change Detection of Disaster Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27018)
*S. Ramírez-Gallego*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于ResNet+FPN的无监督遥感变化检测方法，无需训练即可利用FPN内在先验实现高效异常检测，适用于多地形/传感器场景，显著降低开发成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督模型依赖昂贵标注数据，难以满足卫星实时自主决策需求；RSFMs通过无监督学习实现资源优化，尤其适用于灾害监测等需快速响应的场景。

Method: 采用预训练ResNet作为特征编码器，结合未训练的FPN架构，在潜在空间检测连续轨道影像间的语义偏移；利用FPN固有先验实现免训练的图像级生成与高分辨率映射。

Result: 在保持与定制化模型相当性能的同时，实现跨地形/传感器的泛化能力；相比传统基于patch的训练方法，大幅降低计算开销并提升检测分辨率。

Conclusion: RSFMs通过统一编码器架构和免训练设计，为地球观测提供了高效、可扩展的无监督解决方案，显著减少开发成本并增强系统自主性。

Abstract: Remote Sensing Foundation Models (RSFMs) have emerged as a powerful alternative to supervised models for Earth Observation, allowing satellites to autonomously trigger high-resolution captures or adjust tasking parameters upon detecting an anomaly, thereby maximizing the utility of the mission's limited power and computational resources. RSFMs are versatile, unified encoders that optimize onboard storage for multiple orbital applications while ensuring high-fidelity feature extraction. In particular, unsupervised change detection with RSFMs offers a well-informed and transformative path for disaster monitoring without expensive labels. In this paper, we present a novel unsupervised detection method based on ResNet (RSFM) + FPN which identifies a wide spectrum of anomalies by detecting subtle semantic shifts in the latent space between successive orbital passes. By relying on an untrained FPN architecture and its intrinsic priors, the system achieves efficient image-level generation and higher resolution mapping with minimal effort (training-free) compared to previous proposals (patch-based, trained). And by replacing tailored models with RSFMs, we can achieve comparable results through an approach that eliminates the need for bespoke training and extensive development effort and adds customization, while ensuring high-performance generalization across diverse terrains and sensors.

</details>


### [72] [PanoImager: Geometry-Guided Novel View Synthesis and Reconstruction from Sparse Panoramic Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27071)
*Zhisong Xu,Takeshi Oishi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PanoImager是一个无需SfM的全景3D重建框架，通过结合前馈姿态/深度先验、几何条件扩散视图补全和深度引导的3D高斯优化，在稀疏全景图像下显著提升重建稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SfM/SLAM在旋转主导、弱视差的运动场景下初始化条件差且不可靠，导致稀疏全景图像的3D重建面临挑战。

Method: 将全景图像分解为局部视角，利用前馈姿态/深度先验和几何条件扩散模型生成辅助观测，结合深度引导的3D高斯优化增强跨视角一致性。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，PanoImager在极端稀疏条件下展现出更高的重建稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为SfM/SLAM初始化失败时的离线/背景组件，用于地图优化与场景重建。

Abstract: Panoramic sensing offers wide field-of-view coverage, yet 3D reconstruction from sparse panoramas remains challenging under rotation-dominant, weak-parallax motion. In such regimes, SfM/SLAM initialization is often ill-conditioned and unreliable. We present PanoImager, an SfM-free framework that combines feed-forward pose/depth priors, geometry-conditioned diffusion view completion, and depth-guided 3DGS optimization. Given only a few panoramic images, PanoImager decomposes them into local perspective views, synthesizes auxiliary observations to enrich sparse evidence, and stabilizes Gaussian optimization for improved cross-view consistency. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show improved stability under extreme sparsity, suggesting PanoImager as an offline/background component for map refinement when SfM/SLAM fails to initialize.

</details>


### [73] [Pseudo-Text-Conditioned 3D Grounding DINO for Organ Localization in Abdominal CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27084)
*Siqi Chen,Han Gong,Keyi Hou,Jingxuan Yang,Sheethal Bhat,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CT-3GDINO，一种基于伪文本引导的轻量级3D器官定位模型，在腹部CT上实现多器官检测，为创伤分析提供空间先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的腹部CT器官定位可为下游创伤分析提供关键空间先验信息，现有方法在3D定位精度和效率方面存在不足。

Method: 采用冻结伪文本类令牌替代真实文本编码器，结合Swin3D视觉骨干、双向特征增强、伪文本引导查询选择和跨模态解码器，预测肝、脾、双肾及肠道的归一化3D边界框。

Result: 在193例RSNA/RATIC数据集上，多尺度从头训练模型取得0.5830整体mAP（IoU 0.1-0.7），粗定位性能优异（IoU 0.1时AP=0.9649），但精细对齐能力有限（IoU 0.7时AP=0.1552）。

Conclusion: CT-3GDINO作为首个开源伪文本条件3D器官定位基线，验证了伪文本引导的有效性，未来需发展定位感知预训练、多模态条件增强和损伤特异性检测技术。

Abstract: Reliable organ localization in abdominal CT can provide spatial priors for downstream trauma analysis. We propose CT-3GDINO, a lightweight 3D detector that adapts a Grounding-DINO-style query-based architecture to fixed organ localization using frozen pseudo-text class tokens instead of a real text encoder. The model combines a Swin3D visual backbone, bidirectional feature enhancement, pseudo-text-guided query selection, and a cross-modality decoder to predict normalized 3D boxes for liver, spleen, left kidney, right kidney, and bowel. We train and evaluate on 193 matched RSNA/RATIC CT volumes with segmentation-derived boxes. The best multi-scale model, trained from scratch, achieves 0.5830 overall top-1 class-wise mAP over 3D IoU thresholds from 0.1 to 0.7, outperforming fixed- and trainable-backbone classification-pretrained variants with 0.5570 and 0.4657 mAP. Performance is strong for coarse localization, with 0.9649 AP at IoU 0.1, but remains limited for strict box alignment, with 0.1552 AP at IoU 0.7. These results establish CT-3GDINO as an open-source baseline for pseudo-text-conditioned 3D organ localization and motivate future work on localization-aware pretraining, richer multimodal conditioning, and injury-focused detection.

</details>


### [74] [SubdivAR: Autoregressive Next-Scale Prediction for Neural Mesh Subdivision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27088)
*Huipeng Guo,Zikai Song,Hang Long,Jielei Zhang,Wenbing Li,Junkai Lin,Tianhao Zhao,Jinshen Zhang,Tianle Guo,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SubdivAR是一种基于自回归表示的神经网格细分框架，通过混合拓扑感知Transformer和顶点偏移预测，在几何细节恢复和拓扑保持方面显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则的细分方法易导致过度平滑，而现有神经细分方法受限于局部建模和泛化能力不足，难以处理复杂几何结构。

Method: 提出Mesh Autoregressive Representation (MAR)将多级细分网格序列化为有序尺度序列，结合混合拓扑感知Transformer实现全局语义注意力与拓扑约束局部特征聚合，采用顶点偏移预测范式逐层生成细分结果，并构建FII-40K数据集提供多尺度监督信号。

Result: 在Hausdorff距离和Chamfer距离上分别优于现有方法18.8%和14.2%，在复杂开放表面几何体上展现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SubdivAR通过自回归建模和拓扑感知机制，在保持细分拓扑的同时有效恢复几何细节，为数字资产创建提供了更通用的细分解决方案。

Abstract: Mesh subdivision is a fundamental operation for converting coarse, editable meshes into high-resolution surfaces, with broad applications in digital asset creation. Classical rule-based schemes rely on fixed local refinement rules and often produce over-smoothed surfaces. Recent neural subdivision methods improve detail synthesis, but remain constrained by local modeling and exhibit limited generalizability.
